-- ============================================================
-- USER PERMISSION OVERRIDES (SEC-01, ADR-009)
-- ============================================================
-- Per-user GRANT/REVOKE on top of the fixed role hierarchy.
-- Precedence (enforced in services/api/src/permissions/resolver.ts):
--   restriction (permission_restrictions / REVOKE) > GRANT > role.
-- GRANT and REVOKE for the same key cannot coexist (UNIQUE).
-- OWNER/ADMIN users cannot hold overrides (trigger below + API check).
-- No UPDATE policy: changing an override is DELETE + INSERT (audited).
-- Additive only; agent_area_grants and permission_restrictions are untouched.
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE user_permission_overrides (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::TEXT,
  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  resource TEXT NOT NULL,
  action TEXT NOT NULL,
  effect TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (effect IN ('GRANT', 'REVOKE')),
  reason TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(reason) BETWEEN 1 AND 300),
  created_by_user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

  CONSTRAINT user_permission_overrides_user_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (agency_id, user_id) REFERENCES users (agency_id, id)
    ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT user_permission_overrides_unique UNIQUE (agency_id, user_id, resource, action)
);

CREATE INDEX user_permission_overrides_user_idx
  ON user_permission_overrides (agency_id, user_id);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION user_permission_overrides_reject_privileged()
RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM users
     WHERE agency_id = NEW.agency_id AND id = NEW.user_id AND role IN ('OWNER', 'ADMIN')
  ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'OWNER/ADMIN users cannot hold permission overrides'
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER user_permission_overrides_reject_privileged_trg
  BEFORE INSERT ON user_permission_overrides
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION user_permission_overrides_reject_privileged();

ALTER TABLE user_permission_overrides ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE user_permission_overrides FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY user_permission_overrides_select_tenant ON user_permission_overrides
  FOR SELECT USING (agency_id = current_agency_id());
CREATE POLICY user_permission_overrides_insert_tenant ON user_permission_overrides
  FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (agency_id = current_agency_id());
CREATE POLICY user_permission_overrides_delete_tenant ON user_permission_overrides
  FOR DELETE USING (agency_id = current_agency_id());

REVOKE ALL ON user_permission_overrides FROM PUBLIC;
-- ============================================================
-- CUSTOMER FIELD SETTINGS (Frente 2, ADR-008)
-- ============================================================
-- Per-agency configuration of the customer registration form: which
-- fields are enabled and which are required. The field registry lives in
-- code (services/api/src/customer-field-settings.ts); no row means
-- "enabled, optional". `name` is locked and never stored here.
-- Disabling a field never deletes stored customer data. Additive only.
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE customer_field_settings (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::TEXT,
  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies (id),
  field_key TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (field_key ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$' AND field_key <> 'name'),
  enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  required BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  updated_by_user_id TEXT,
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

  CONSTRAINT customer_field_settings_required_enabled_check CHECK (NOT required OR enabled),
  CONSTRAINT customer_field_settings_unique UNIQUE (agency_id, field_key)
);

ALTER TABLE customer_field_settings ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE customer_field_settings FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

  FOR SELECT USING (agency_id = current_agency_id());
CREATE POLICY customer_field_settings_insert_tenant ON customer_field_settings
  FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (agency_id = current_agency_id());
CREATE POLICY customer_field_settings_update_tenant ON customer_field_settings
  FOR UPDATE USING (agency_id = current_agency_id()) WITH CHECK (agency_id = current_agency_id());

REVOKE ALL ON customer_field_settings FROM PUBLIC;
-- ============================================================
-- 108_commission_release_policy.sql
-- Onda E2 (COM-03 + Frente 8): liberacao de comissao por recebimento. ADITIVA.
--   * payment_method_receipt_rules: a FORMA de pagamento define SE a venda exige o dinheiro recebido
--     (requires_receipt). Ausencia de linha = regra nunca decidida (tri-estado) -> recurso BLOQUEADO.

export async function cancelRevenueEntry(database: DatabaseRuntime, entryId: string) {
  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const agencyId = getAgencyId();
    await lockEntrySale(client, agencyId, entryId);
    const entry = await client.query<EntryRow>(
      `SELECT id, sale_item_id, status, entitlement_event, amount FROM agency_revenue_entries
        WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2 FOR UPDATE`,
      [getAgencyId(), entryId],
    );
    const row = entry.rows[0];
    if (!row) throw new NotFoundError('Revenue entry not found');
    if (row.status === 'RECEIVED') throw new ConflictError('A received entry cannot be cancelled');
    if (row.status === 'CANCELLED') return { id: row.id, status: 'CANCELLED' as const };
    await setStatus(client, row.id, 'CANCELLED', AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_CANCELLED);
    return { id: row.id, status: 'CANCELLED' as const };
  });
}

interface EntryView {
  id: string;
  sale_item_id: string;
  supplier_id: string | null;
  rule_version_id: string | null;
  status: EntryStatus;
  entitlement_event: EntitlementEvent | null;
  amount: number;
  base_amount: number;
  calc_version: number;
  memory: Record<string, unknown>;
  entitled_at: Date | null;
  received_at: Date | null;
  received_amount: number | null;
}

async function loadEntries(client: TenantTransactionClient, saleId: string) {
  const result = await client.query<EntryView>(
    `SELECT id, sale_item_id, supplier_id, rule_version_id, status, entitlement_event, amount::float8 AS amount,
            base_amount::float8 AS base_amount, calc_version, memory, entitled_at, received_at, received_amount::float8 AS received_amount
       FROM agency_revenue_entries WHERE agency_id = $1 AND sale_id = $2 ORDER BY created_at, id`,
    [getAgencyId(), saleId],
  );
  return result.rows;
}

/** Read model: revenue entries plus an analysis-only comparison with the employee commission (no netting). */
export async function getSaleRevenue(database: DatabaseRuntime, saleId: string) {
  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const sale = await client.query(`SELECT 1 FROM sales WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2`, [getAgencyId(), saleId]);
    if (sale.rowCount === 0) throw new NotFoundError('Sale not found');
    const entries = await loadEntries(client, saleId);
    const commission = await client.query<{ total: string }>(
      `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS total FROM commission_entries
        WHERE agency_id = $1 AND sale_id = $2 AND status <> 'CANCELLED'`,
      [getAgencyId(), saleId],
    );
    const sum = (statuses: string[], field: 'amount' | 'received_amount' = 'amount') =>
      Math.round(entries.filter((entry) => statuses.includes(entry.status as string)).reduce((total, entry) => total + Math.round(Number(entry[field] ?? 0) * 100), 0)) / 100;
    const projected = sum(['FORECAST', 'ENTITLED', 'AVAILABLE', 'RECEIVED']);
    const expectedReceived = sum(['RECEIVED']);
    const actualReceived = sum(['RECEIVED'], 'received_amount');
    const employeeCommission = Math.round(Number(commission.rows[0]?.total ?? 0) * 100) / 100;
    return {
      entries,
      totals: {
        forecast: sum(['FORECAST']),
        entitled: sum(['ENTITLED', 'AVAILABLE']),
        expected: expectedReceived,
        received: actualReceived,
        difference: Math.round((expectedReceived - actualReceived) * 100) / 100,
        pendingRule: entries.filter((entry) => entry.status === 'PENDING_RULE').length,
      },
      // Analysis only (D3): two independent obligations, never netted or linked by payment.
      analysis: {
        agencyRevenue: projected,
        employeeCommission,
        difference: Math.round((projected - employeeCommission) * 100) / 100,
      },
    };
  });
}
// PermissionRestriction (Agent 01 SaaS Admin).
//
// Additive-only, tenant-scoped narrowing of what a role may do WITHIN
// THAT TENANT. This is layered ON TOP OF requireRole() -- never a
// replacement for it -- and can only take permissions away, never grant
// permissions the base RBAC hierarchy (OWNER>ADMIN>MANAGER>AGENT>VIEWER)
// would not already allow. See governance/AUTONOMY.md and
// security/NON_NEGOTIABLES.md: RBAC base meanings must never change.
//
// Guardrails enforced here (not just at the DB layer):
//   * OWNER and ADMIN can never be restricted -- prevents a tenant from
//     locking itself out of its own agency (self-lockout). Enforced by
//     both the CHECK constraint on permission_restrictions.role and the
//     assertion in createPermissionRestriction() below.
//   * assertNotRestricted() is called AFTER requireRole() at each route
//     boundary that opts in -- it can only reject a request that RBAC
//     already would have allowed; it can never let through a request
//     RBAC denies (there is no bypass path here at all).
import { getAgencyId, getUserId, requireRole } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from './database';
import { ForbiddenError } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { ConflictError, ValidationError } from './errors';
import { AuditEventType, recordAuditEvent } from './audit-log';

export interface PermissionRestriction {
  id: string;
  agencyId: string;
  role: UserRole;
  resource: string;
  action: string;
  createdByUserId: string;
  createdAt: Date;
}

interface PermissionRestrictionRow {
  id: string;
  agency_id: string;
  role: UserRole;
  resource: string;
  action: string;
  created_by_user_id: string;
  created_at: string;
}

// Roles a tenant is allowed to restrict. OWNER/ADMIN are intentionally
// excluded -- restricting them would let a tenant lock itself out of its
// own agency, which this mechanism must never permit.
const RESTRICTABLE_ROLES: ReadonlySet<UserRole> = new Set([
  UserRole.MANAGER,
  UserRole.AGENT,
  UserRole.VIEWER,
]);

function toPermissionRestriction(row: PermissionRestrictionRow): PermissionRestriction {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    agencyId: row.agency_id,
    role: row.role,
    resource: row.resource,
    action: row.action,
    createdByUserId: row.created_by_user_id,
    createdAt: new Date(row.created_at),
  };
}

export interface CreatePermissionRestrictionInput {
  role: UserRole;
  resource: string;
  action: string;
}

export async function createPermissionRestriction(
  database: DatabaseRuntime,
  input: CreatePermissionRestrictionInput,
): Promise<PermissionRestriction> {
  // Only OWNER/ADMIN may configure restrictions for their tenant.
  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();
  const createdByUserId = getUserId();

  if (!RESTRICTABLE_ROLES.has(input.role)) {
    throw new ValidationError(
      'Apenas os papÃ©is MANAGER, AGENT e VIEWER podem ser restringidos. OWNER e ADMIN nunca podem ser restringidos (previne bloqueio do prÃ³prio tenant).',
    );
  }
  const resource = input.resource?.trim();
  const action = input.action?.trim();
  if (!resource || !action) {
    throw new ValidationError('resource e action sÃ£o obrigatÃ³rios');
  }

  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    let result;
    try {
      result = await client.query<PermissionRestrictionRow>(
        `INSERT INTO permission_restrictions (agency_id, role, resource, action, created_by_user_id)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
         RETURNING id, agency_id, role, resource, action, created_by_user_id, created_at`,
        [agencyId, input.role, resource, action, createdByUserId],
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (isUniqueViolation(error)) {
        throw new ConflictError('Esta restriÃ§Ã£o jÃ¡ existe para este papel/recurso/aÃ§Ã£o');
      }
      throw error;
    }
    const row = result.rows[0];
    if (!row) {
      throw new Error('Permission restriction insert did not return a row');
    }
    const created = toPermissionRestriction(row);
    await recordAuditEvent(client, {
      eventType: AuditEventType.PERMISSION_RESTRICTION_CREATED,
      entityType: 'permission_restriction',
      entityId: created.id,
      metadata: { role: created.role, resource: created.resource, action: created.action },
    });
    return created;
  });
}

export async function listPermissionRestrictions(
  database: DatabaseRuntime,
): Promise<PermissionRestriction[]> {
  requireRole(UserRole.VIEWER);
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();

  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const result = await client.query<PermissionRestrictionRow>(
      `SELECT id, agency_id, role, resource, action, created_by_user_id, created_at
       FROM permission_restrictions WHERE agency_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC`,
      [agencyId],
    );
    return result.rows.map(toPermissionRestriction);
  });
}

export async function deletePermissionRestriction(
  database: DatabaseRuntime,
  id: string,
): Promise<boolean> {
  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();

  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const result = await client.query<{ id: string; role: UserRole; resource: string; action: string }>(
      `DELETE FROM permission_restrictions WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2
       RETURNING id, role, resource, action`,
      [agencyId, id],
    );
    const row = result.rows[0];
    if (!row) {
      return false;
    }
    await recordAuditEvent(client, {
      eventType: AuditEventType.PERMISSION_RESTRICTION_DELETED,
      entityType: 'permission_restriction',
      entityId: row.id,
      metadata: { role: row.role, resource: row.resource, action: row.action },
    });
    return true;
  });
}

// ============================================================
// Enforcement helper -- call AFTER requireRole() at a route boundary.
// ============================================================
//
// This can only ever narrow: it looks up whether the CURRENT actor's
// role has been restricted, for this tenant, from (resource, action).
// It never consults or overrides requireRole() -- a caller whose role
// fails requireRole() never reaches this check at all, and a caller who
// passes requireRole() can still be blocked here if their tenant chose
// to restrict it. OWNER/ADMIN rows can never exist in the table (DB
// CHECK constraint + createPermissionRestriction() validation), so this
// can never block an OWNER/ADMIN regardless of what is queried.
export async function assertNotRestricted(
  client: TenantTransactionClient,
  role: UserRole,
  resource: string,
  action: string,
): Promise<void> {
  if (role === UserRole.OWNER || role === UserRole.ADMIN) {
    return;
  }
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();
  const result = await client.query<{ id: string }>(
    `SELECT id FROM permission_restrictions
     WHERE agency_id = $1 AND role = $2 AND resource = $3 AND action = $4
     LIMIT 1`,
    [agencyId, role, resource, action],
  );
  if (result.rows.length > 0) {
    throw new ForbiddenError(
      `Esta aÃ§Ã£o (${resource}:${action}) foi restringida para o papel ${role} nesta agÃªncia`,
    );
  }
}

function isUniqueViolation(error: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    typeof error === 'object' &&
    error !== null &&
    'code' in error &&
    (error as { code?: unknown }).code === '23505'
  );
}
import type { FastifyInstance, preHandlerHookHandler } from 'fastify';
import { requireRole } from '../../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { UserRole } from '../../../../packages/domain/types';
import {
  calculateRevenueForSale,
  cancelRevenueEntry,
  createRuleVersion,
  getSaleRevenue,
  listRuleVersions,
  parseRuleInput,
  receiveRevenueEntry,
  reactivateRevenueEntry,
} from '../agency-revenue';
import type { DatabaseRuntime } from '../database';
import { ValidationError } from '../errors';

export function registerAgencyRevenueRoutes(
  app: FastifyInstance,
  options: { database: DatabaseRuntime; protectedHooks: preHandlerHookHandler[] },
): void {
  const { database, protectedHooks } = options;

  app.get('/agency-revenue/rules', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async () => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    return { rules: await listRuleVersions(database) };
  });

  app.post('/agency-revenue/rules', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request, reply) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    const created = await createRuleVersion(database, parseRuleInput(request.body));
    reply.code(201);
    return { rule: created };
  });

  app.get<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/sales/:id/agency-revenue', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    return getSaleRevenue(database, request.params.id);
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/sales/:id/agency-revenue/calculate', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    return calculateRevenueForSale(database, request.params.id);
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/agency-revenue/entries/:id/receive', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    const body = request.body;
    if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Request body must be an object');
    const record = body as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (Object.keys(record).some((key) => !['amount', 'reference'].includes(key))) throw new ValidationError('Unknown field in request body');
    if (typeof record.amount !== 'number') throw new ValidationError('Field "amount" must be a number');
    if (record.reference !== undefined && (typeof record.reference !== 'string' || record.reference.length > 200)) {
      throw new ValidationError('Field "reference" is invalid');
    }
    return receiveRevenueEntry(database, request.params.id, {
      amount: record.amount,
      reference: typeof record.reference === 'string' ? record.reference : null,
    });
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/agency-revenue/entries/:id/cancel', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    return cancelRevenueEntry(database, request.params.id);
  });
  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/agency-revenue/entries/:id/reactivate', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    const body = request.body;
    if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Request body must be an object');
    const record = body as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (Object.keys(record).some((key) => key !== 'reason') || typeof record.reason !== 'string') {
      throw new ValidationError('A reason is required');
    }
    return reactivateRevenueEntry(database, request.params.id, record.reason);
  });
}
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  projectCommissions,
  projectReceivables,
  projectRevenue,
  supplierBalances,
  type CommissionRow,
  type ReceivableRow,
  type RevenueRow,
} from '../src/report-projections';

const receivable = (over: Partial<ReceivableRow>): ReceivableRow => ({
  id: 'r',
  saleId: 's',
  dueDate: '2026-11-10',
  amount: 100,
  paid: 0,
  status: 'OPEN',
  ...over,
});

describe('projectReceivables (customer receivables by due date)', () => {
  it('open balance is amount minus allocations, bucketed by due date, late separated', () => {
    const result = projectReceivables(
      [
        receivable({ id: 'a', dueDate: '2026-10-20', amount: 500, paid: 200, status: 'PARTIALLY_PAID' }),
        receivable({ id: 'b', dueDate: '2026-11-10', amount: 300 }),
        receivable({ id: 'c', dueDate: '2026-11-25', amount: 200, paid: 50, status: 'PARTIALLY_PAID' }),
        receivable({ id: 'd', dueDate: '2026-12-05', amount: 100 }),
      ],
      '2026-10-24',
    );
    expect(result.late).toEqual({ count: 1, openBalance: 300 });
    expect(result.byMonth).toEqual([
      { month: '2026-11', openBalance: 450, count: 2 },
      { month: '2026-12', openBalance: 100, count: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(result.totalOpen).toBe(850);
  });

  it('a due date equal to today is not late; settled and cancelled rows are ignored', () => {
    const result = projectReceivables(
      [
        receivable({ id: 'a', dueDate: '2026-10-24', amount: 100 }),
        receivable({ id: 'b', dueDate: '2026-10-01', amount: 100, paid: 100, status: 'PAID' }),
        receivable({ id: 'c', dueDate: '2026-10-01', amount: 100, status: 'CANCELLED' }),
      ],
      '2026-10-24',
    );
    expect(result.late).toEqual({ count: 0, openBalance: 0 });
    expect(result.byMonth).toEqual([{ month: '2026-10', openBalance: 100, count: 1 }]);
    expect(result.totalOpen).toBe(100);
  });

  it('works in cents (no floating point drift)', () => {
    const result = projectReceivables(
      [receivable({ amount: 0.3, paid: 0.1 }), receivable({ id: 'x', amount: 0.2 })],
      '2026-10-24',
    );
    expect(result.totalOpen).toBe(0.4);
  });
});

const revenue = (over: Partial<RevenueRow>): RevenueRow => ({
  entryId: 'e',
  supplierId: 's1',
  status: 'FORECAST',
  amount: 100,
  receivedAmount: null,
  entitledOn: null,
  expectedOn: null,
  ...over,
});

describe('projectRevenue (contracted / projected / undefined; never PENDING_RULE)', () => {
  it('classifies ENTITLED/AVAILABLE as CONTRATADO, FORECAST with a date as PROJETADO, FORECAST without as INDEFINIDO', () => {
    const result = projectRevenue([
      revenue({ entryId: '1', status: 'ENTITLED', amount: 70, entitledOn: '2026-10-20' }),
      revenue({ entryId: '2', status: 'ENTITLED', amount: 45, entitledOn: '2026-10-22' }),
      revenue({ entryId: '3', status: 'AVAILABLE', amount: 120, entitledOn: '2026-10-25' }),
      revenue({ entryId: '4', status: 'FORECAST', amount: 80, expectedOn: '2026-12-02' }),
      revenue({ entryId: '5', status: 'FORECAST', amount: 60 }),
    ]);
    expect(result.contracted.total).toBe(235);
    expect(result.projected.total).toBe(80);
    expect(result.undefined.total).toBe(60);
    expect(result.contracted.byMonth).toEqual([{ month: '2026-10', amount: 235 }]);
    expect(result.projected.byMonth).toEqual([{ month: '2026-12', amount: 80 }]);
    expect(result.grandTotal).toBe(375);
  });

  it('never counts PENDING_RULE, CANCELLED or RECEIVED entries', () => {
    const result = projectRevenue([
      revenue({ entryId: '1', status: 'PENDING_RULE', amount: 0 }),
      revenue({ entryId: '2', status: 'CANCELLED', amount: 50 }),
      revenue({ entryId: '3', status: 'RECEIVED', amount: 90, receivedAmount: 90 }),
      revenue({ entryId: '4', status: 'ENTITLED', amount: 10, entitledOn: '2026-10-20' }),
    ]);
    expect(result.grandTotal).toBe(10);
  });

import { AsyncLocalStorage } from 'node:async_hooks';
import type { HookHandlerDoneFunction } from 'fastify';
import type { TenantContext } from './types';
import { UserRole } from './types';

export class TenantError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public code: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'TenantError';
  }
}

export class UnauthorizedError extends TenantError {
  constructor(message = 'Unauthorized') {
    super(message, 'UNAUTHORIZED');
    this.name = 'UnauthorizedError';
  }
}

export class ForbiddenError extends TenantError {
  constructor(message = 'Forbidden: No access to this agency') {
    super(message, 'FORBIDDEN');
    this.name = 'ForbiddenError';
  }
}

export interface AuthPayload {
  sub: string;
  agency_id: string;
  role: UserRole;
  email: string;
  iat?: number;
  exp?: number;
}

export type ValidateUserAgencyAccess = (
  userId: string,
  agencyId: string,
) => Promise<boolean>;

export interface TenantContextHookOptions {
  validateUserAgencyAccess: ValidateUserAgencyAccess;
}

export type TenantFastifyRequest = {
  auth?: AuthPayload;
  body?: unknown;
};

// ============================================================
// CUSTOMER PORTAL TENANT CONTEXT
// Fully separate from the staff auth/context flow above. Never merged
// with AuthPayload/establishTenantContext -- a customer identity must
// never be mistaken for (or escalate into) a staff principal.
// ============================================================

export interface CustomerAuthPayload {
  agencyId: string;
  customerId: string;
}

// Independent DB-backed check that the resolved customerId actually
// belongs to the resolved agencyId (mirrors ValidateUserAgencyAccess /
// validateUserAgencyAccess for the staff flow). Must query the
// `customers` table -- never trust the auth layer's claim alone.
export type ValidateCustomerAgencyAccess = (
  customerId: string,
  agencyId: string,
) => Promise<boolean>;

export interface CustomerTenantContextHookOptions {
  validateCustomerAgencyAccess: ValidateCustomerAgencyAccess;
}

export type CustomerTenantFastifyRequest = {
  customerAuth?: CustomerAuthPayload;
  body?: unknown;
};

// Synthetic, non-secret placeholders used only to satisfy
// assertValidTenantContext's staff-shaped invariants (non-empty
// userId/userRole) for a customer-portal request. Never treated as a
// real user id or role: customer-portal routes never call
// requireRole()/requireExactRole() and never read userId for anything
// but this context's own internal consistency.
const CUSTOMER_CONTEXT_ROLE = UserRole.VIEWER;

export async function establishCustomerTenantContext(
  request: CustomerTenantFastifyRequest,
  options: CustomerTenantContextHookOptions,
): Promise<TenantContext> {
  const customerAuth = request.customerAuth;

  if (!customerAuth) {
    throw new UnauthorizedError('Customer authentication required');
  }

  if (!isNonEmptyString(customerAuth.agencyId) || !isNonEmptyString(customerAuth.customerId)) {
    throw new UnauthorizedError('Customer authentication required');
  }

  const belongsToAgency = await options.validateCustomerAgencyAccess(
    customerAuth.customerId,
    customerAuth.agencyId,
  );

  if (!belongsToAgency) {
    throw new ForbiddenError('Customer does not belong to this agency');
  }

  const context: TenantContext = {
    agencyId: customerAuth.agencyId,
    userId: `customer-context:${customerAuth.customerId}`,
    userRole: CUSTOMER_CONTEXT_ROLE,
    email: '',
    customerId: customerAuth.customerId,
  };

  assertValidTenantContext(context);
  return context;
}

export function createCustomerTenantContextHook(options: CustomerTenantContextHookOptions) {
  return (
    request: CustomerTenantFastifyRequest,
    reply: TenantFastifyReply,
    done: HookHandlerDoneFunction,
  ): void => {
    establishCustomerTenantContext(request, options)
      .then((context) => {
        tenantStorage.run(context, done);
      })
      .catch((error: unknown) => {
        sendTenantError(reply, error);
      });
  };
}

// ============================================================
// PARTNER PORTAL TENANT CONTEXT
// Fully separate from the staff auth/context flow AND from the customer
// portal flow above. A partner (external affiliate/agent) is not a
// `users` row with a staff role, and never a `customers` row either --
// separate types, separate hook, separate request decoration
// (`partnerAuth`, not `auth`/`customerAuth`). Mirrors the customer-portal
// pattern exactly, per AGENT_04_PARTNERS mission guidance.
// ============================================================

export interface PartnerAuthPayload {
  agencyId: string;
  partnerId: string;
}

// Independent DB-backed check that the resolved partnerId actually
// belongs to the resolved agencyId (mirrors ValidateCustomerAgencyAccess).
// Must query the `commercial_partners` table -- never trust the auth
// layer's claim alone.
export type ValidatePartnerAgencyAccess = (
  partnerId: string,
  agencyId: string,
) => Promise<boolean>;

export interface PartnerTenantContextHookOptions {
  validatePartnerAgencyAccess: ValidatePartnerAgencyAccess;
}

export type PartnerTenantFastifyRequest = {
  partnerAuth?: PartnerAuthPayload;
  body?: unknown;
};

// Synthetic, non-secret placeholder, same rationale as CUSTOMER_CONTEXT_ROLE:
import type { Pool, PoolClient, QueryResult, QueryResultRow } from 'pg';
import { getTenantContext } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';

export interface TenantTransactionClient {
  query<T extends QueryResultRow = QueryResultRow>(
    text: string,
    values?: readonly unknown[],
  ): Promise<QueryResult<T>>;
}

export interface DatabaseRuntime {
  withTenantTransaction<T>(
    operation: (client: TenantTransactionClient) => Promise<T>,
  ): Promise<T>;
  withPlatformTransaction<T>(
    operation: (client: TenantTransactionClient) => Promise<T>,
  ): Promise<T>;
}

// ============================================================
// PLATFORM-SCOPED RUNTIME
// Deliberately separate from DatabaseRuntime.withTenantTransaction(),
// which always pulls agency_id/user_id from the AMBIENT request tenant
// context (getTenantContext()). This runtime instead takes an EXPLICIT
// target agencyId supplied by the caller, so it can only ever be reached
// from a narrow, explicitly platform-scoped code path (see
// entitlements.ts / the /platform/entitlements route) -- never from
// ordinary agency-authenticated request handling. See entitlements.ts
// for the full "why" (Offer & Growth Engine batch, section H: there is
// no real platform-admin identity in this codebase yet -- this is a
// documented temporary stopgap, not a real security boundary).
// ============================================================

export interface PlatformDatabaseRuntime {
  withAgencyTransaction<T>(
    targetAgencyId: string,
    actorLabel: string | null,
    operation: (client: TenantTransactionClient) => Promise<T>,
  ): Promise<T>;
  /**
   * Runs `operation` with no tenant context, but with the given Postgres
   * GUC set to `lookupValue` for the duration of the transaction. Exists
   * for the "resolve a row by a caller-supplied opaque value before any
   * tenant context can be established" pattern used throughout this
   * codebase (invitations' app.invitation_lookup_hash, enrollment's
   * app.enrollment_lookup_hash, and local-auth.ts's session/password-reset
   * token lookups and agency-slug resolution) -- the matching RLS policy
   * on the target table only ever exposes the single row whose column
   * equals this session-local value, never a scan.
   */
  withPublicLookupTransaction<T>(
    gucName: string,
    lookupValue: string,
    operation: (client: TenantTransactionClient) => Promise<T>,
  ): Promise<T>;
}

export function createPlatformDatabaseRuntime(pool: Pool): PlatformDatabaseRuntime {
  return {
    async withAgencyTransaction<T>(
      targetAgencyId: string,
      actorLabel: string | null,
      operation: (client: TenantTransactionClient) => Promise<T>,
    ): Promise<T> {
      const client = await pool.connect();

      try {
        await client.query('BEGIN');
        await client.query('SELECT set_tenant_context($1, $2)', [targetAgencyId, actorLabel]);

        const result = await operation(createTransactionClient(client));
        await client.query('COMMIT');
        return result;
      } catch (error: unknown) {
        await rollbackQuietly(client);
        throw error;
      } finally {
        client.release();
      }
    },

    async withPublicLookupTransaction<T>(
      gucName: string,
      lookupValue: string,
      operation: (client: TenantTransactionClient) => Promise<T>,
    ): Promise<T> {
      const client = await pool.connect();

      try {
        await client.query('BEGIN');
        await client.query('SELECT set_config($1, $2, true)', [gucName, lookupValue]);

        const result = await operation(createTransactionClient(client));
        await client.query('COMMIT');
        return result;
      } catch (error: unknown) {
        await rollbackQuietly(client);
        throw error;
      } finally {
        client.release();
      }
    },
  };
}

/**
 * Dual-pool runtime (F-06): tenant operations run on the runtime role
 * (RLS-backed, no platform-table grants); platform operations run on the
 * platform role (superset). `platformPool` defaults to `tenantPool` so
 * single-pool unit/integration tests that only exercise tenant paths keep
 * working unchanged â€” production and any suite that hits platform tables
 * must pass a real platform-role pool (see server.ts / PLATFORM_DATABASE_URL).
 */
export function createDatabaseRuntime(tenantPool: Pool, platformPool: Pool = tenantPool): DatabaseRuntime {
  return {
    async withTenantTransaction<T>(
      operation: (client: TenantTransactionClient) => Promise<T>,
    ): Promise<T> {
      const context = getTenantContext();
      const client = await tenantPool.connect();

function readSql(filePath: string): string {
  return readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
}

function readAllMigrations(): string {
  const migrationFiles = readdirSync(migrationsDir)
    .filter((fileName) => /^\d+_.+\.sql$/.test(fileName))
    .sort();

  expect(migrationFiles.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(15);
  migrationFiles.forEach((fileName, index) => {
    expect(fileName.startsWith(`${String(index + 1).padStart(3, '0')}_`)).toBe(true);
  });

  return migrationFiles
    .map((fileName) => readSql(resolve(migrationsDir, fileName)))
    .join('\n');
}

name: systematic-debugging
description: Use when encountering any bug, test failure, or unexpected behavior, before proposing fixes
---

# Systematic Debugging

## Overview

Random fixes waste time and create new bugs. Quick patches mask underlying issues.

**Core principle:** ALWAYS find root cause before attempting fixes. Symptom fixes are failure.

**Violating the letter of this process is violating the spirit of debugging.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO FIXES WITHOUT ROOT CAUSE INVESTIGATION FIRST
```

If you haven't completed Phase 1, you cannot propose fixes.

## When to Use

Use for ANY technical issue:
- Test failures
- Bugs in production
- Unexpected behavior
- Performance problems
- Build failures
- Integration issues

**Use this ESPECIALLY when:**
- Under time pressure (emergencies make guessing tempting)
- "Just one quick fix" seems obvious
- You've already tried multiple fixes
- Previous fix didn't work
- You don't fully understand the issue

**Don't skip when:**
- Issue seems simple (simple bugs have root causes too)
- You're in a hurry (rushing guarantees rework)
- Manager wants it fixed NOW (systematic is faster than thrashing)

## The Four Phases

You MUST complete each phase before proceeding to the next.

### Phase 1: Root Cause Investigation

**BEFORE attempting ANY fix:**

1. **Read Error Messages Carefully**
   - Don't skip past errors or warnings
   - They often contain the exact solution
   - Read stack traces completely
   - Note line numbers, file paths, error codes

2. **Reproduce Consistently**
   - Can you trigger it reliably?
   - What are the exact steps?
   - Does it happen every time?
   - If not reproducible â†’ gather more data, don't guess

# ADR-007: DomÃ­nio "receita da agÃªncia" (separado de comissÃ£o de funcionÃ¡rio, valor vendido e repasse)

## Status

**Aceito pelo usuÃ¡rio em 2026-10-23**, como rascunhado, com D1 e D3 nos padrÃµes
propostos (AGENCY_COLLECTS por padrÃ£o; comissÃ£o do funcionÃ¡rio independente do
recebimento do fornecedor). Antes de implementar: a Fase 0 de comissÃµes precisa
estar integrada e validada, e nenhuma migration Ã© aplicada em banco real sem
autorizaÃ§Ã£o especÃ­fica.

## Metadados

- **Origem:** `.claude/ESPECIFICACAO-AJUSTES-GADOTTI-2026-10-23.md`, Â§2.1â€“Â§2.2,
  Â§8 (Frente 6), Â§9 (Frente 7), Â§10 (Frente 8), Â§11 (Frente 9) e Â§13 (Onda 0, item b).
- **DecisÃµes de referÃªncia:** Gadotti D1 (arrecadaÃ§Ã£o), D3 (comissÃ£o do
  funcionÃ¡rio nÃ£o depende do fornecedor), D4 (arredondamento), D6 (cancelamento
  com receita recebida), D7 (lotes e janelas). A nomenclatura segue a spec Â§14.
- **Relacionados:** ADR-006 (cronograma e quitaÃ§Ã£o do cliente), ADR-008
  (passageiros e campos de venda), DEC-007, ADR-005.
- **RevisÃ£o Codex:** nÃ£o ocorreu.

## Contexto

- **NÃ£o existe receita da agÃªncia em nenhum dos produtos.** O Lite tem apenas
  `seller_commissions` (comissÃ£o do vendedor). O Full tem apenas
  `commission_entries` e `employee_commission_rules` (comissÃ£o do funcionÃ¡rio)
  (spec Â§8; `COMMISSION-ENGINE-SPEC.md` Â§1; `employee-commissions.ts`).
- `TravelProduct.commissionPercent` no Full Ã© um campo **armazenado e validado,
  mas nÃ£o consumido** por nenhum cÃ¡lculo (grep em `services/api/src`, spec Â§5).
  Trata-se de dado Ã³rfÃ£o.
- `SaleItem.commission` no Full: tipo verificado, semÃ¢ntica de uso **nÃ£o
  verificada** (spec Â§15).
- A Gadotti Ã© a agÃªncia que revende. Ela recebe **% sobre o total**, **% sobre
  parte** (taxas, tarifas), **valor fixo por passageiro** (Ã´nibus intermunicipal),
  **sÃ³ sobre taxas** (cruzeiro) e **vÃ¡rios fornecedores com percentuais
  distintos** (aÃ©reo e pacotes). Esses sÃ£o valores de polÃ­tica comercial do
  fornecedor, nÃ£o regras legais. Devem ser configuraÃ§Ã£o, sem hardcode.
- O relato separa duas linhas de dinheiro: a **receita da agÃªncia** (o fornecedor
  paga Ã  agÃªncia) e a **comissÃ£o do funcionÃ¡rio** (a agÃªncia paga ao vendedor).
  Elas sÃ£o obrigaÃ§Ãµes diferentes e nÃ£o podem compartilhar tabela de regra.

## DecisÃ£o proposta

### 1. Cinco grandezas, cada uma com o seu lugar

| Grandeza | DefiniÃ§Ã£o | Onde vive (proposta) | NÃ£o confundir com |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Valor vendido (meta) | total vendido ao cliente | `sales.gross_amount` (Lite) / `sales.total` (Full) | receita |
| Componentes | tarifa, taxas, hospedagem, seguro | `sale_items` (Full jÃ¡ tem; Lite ganha, ADR-008) | total |
| Receita da agÃªncia | comissÃ£o devida **Ã  agÃªncia** pelo fornecedor | `agency_revenue_entries` (novo) | margem, comissÃ£o do funcionÃ¡rio |
| Repasse ao fornecedor | custo pago ao fornecedor | `payables` / `sale_cost_items` (Lite); `payables` (Full) | receita |
| ComissÃ£o do funcionÃ¡rio | obrigaÃ§Ã£o da agÃªncia com o vendedor | `seller_commissions` (Lite) / `commission_entries` (Full) | receita |

- RelatÃ³rios de resultado somam **receita ou margem**, nunca as duas.
- `margem = vendido âˆ’ custo` sÃ³ existe no modelo em que a agÃªncia cobra tudo e
  repassa (`AGENCY_COLLECTS`). No modelo `SUPPLIER_COLLECTS` existe apenas receita.
  (Ver Gadotti D1 abaixo.)

### 2. TrÃªs eventos distintos (nenhum implica o outro)

| Evento | Significa | Estado da entrada |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Direito** | a receita passou a ser devida | `ENTITLED` (com `entitled_at`) |
| **Disponibilidade** | a receita entrou num lote ou janela para cobranÃ§a do fornecedor | `AVAILABLE` |
| **LiquidaÃ§Ã£o** | o fornecedor pagou de fato a agÃªncia | `RECEIVED` |

- QuitaÃ§Ã£o do cliente nÃ£o Ã© liquidaÃ§Ã£o do fornecedor. AprovaÃ§Ã£o de cartÃ£o nÃ£o Ã©
  dinheiro recebido; o cadastro da forma de pagamento pode tratÃ¡-la como quitada
  apenas para fins de direito (ver item 6).

### 3. Regra de receita versionada (mesmo padrÃ£o da COM-01, sem reaproveitar tabelas de comissÃ£o)

```text
agency_revenue_scopes(id, scope_owner_id, supplier_id NULL, product_id NULL,
                      category_id NULL, status, legacy_ref)
  precedÃªncia: produto+fornecedor > produto > fornecedor+categoria
               > fornecedor > categoria > padrÃ£o da agÃªncia

agency_revenue_rule_versions(id, scope_id, version_number, effective_from DATE,
  revised_at, revised_by, reason,
  mode           -- PERCENTAGE | FIXED_PER_PASSENGER | FIXED_PER_SALE
                 --   | FIXED_PER_ITEM | NONE
  base_selector  -- LINE_TOTAL | NET_FARE | TAXES | FEES | TAXES_AND_FEES | MARGIN
                 --   (somente para PERCENTAGE)
  value NUMERIC(14,4),
  entitlement_event,       -- ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION | ON_FULL_SETTLEMENT
                           --   | ON_BALANCE_DEADLINE
  settlement_policy_id,
  calc_version)
```

- VersÃµes sÃ£o **append-only**. Regra nova nÃ£o altera entradas existentes. Regra
  retroativa nÃ£o Ã© permitida nesta versÃ£o (mesma polÃ­tica do Lite,
  `assertNotRetroactive`).
- VigÃªncia Ã© uma coluna (`effective_from`) e a projeÃ§Ã£o Ã© calculada. NÃ£o se usa
  `valid_until` derivado (defeito R11 do relatÃ³rio de paridade).

### 4. CÃ¡lculo por item e por fornecedor

- Bases, por item (`SaleItem`):
  - `LINE_TOTAL` = qty Ã— preÃ§o âˆ’ desconto + taxas + fees;
  - `NET_FARE` = qty Ã— preÃ§o âˆ’ desconto;
  - `TAXES`, `FEES`, `TAXES_AND_FEES`;
  - `MARGIN` (definiÃ§Ã£o pendente, Q4).
- `receita_item = round_half_up(base_item Ã— taxa / 100, 2)`;
  `receita_venda = Î£ receita_item`.
- Cada item gera uma linha em `agency_revenue_entries` com **snapshot completo**:
  versÃ£o, base, taxa, memÃ³ria de cÃ¡lculo e `calc_version`.
- Fixo por passageiro usa `passenger_count` da venda ou do item (ADR-008).
- **Item sem fornecedor ou sem regra aplicÃ¡vel** gera entrada `PENDING_RULE`:
  valor 0, fora de qualquer relatÃ³rio de receita reconhecida.
- Arredondamento (Gadotti D4): para a **receita da agÃªncia**, half-up
  decimal-safe por item, igual ao `roundMoney` do Lite. Para a **comissÃ£o do
  funcionÃ¡rio**, vale o arredondamento atual de cada produto (Gadotti F4, sem
  mudanÃ§a).

### 5. Estados e transiÃ§Ãµes

- `FORECAST` â†’ `ENTITLED` â†’ `AVAILABLE` â†’ `RECEIVED`, com `CANCELLED` possÃ­vel
  antes de `RECEIVED`.
- TransiÃ§Ãµes sÃ³ no servidor, por uma funÃ§Ã£o Ãºnica de domÃ­nio, com auditoria.
- **Modelo de arrecadaÃ§Ã£o (Gadotti D1)**: parÃ¢metro `collection_model` por
  fornecedor ou produto. `AGENCY_COLLECTS` Ã© o padrÃ£o proposto (igual ao Lite
  hoje). `SUPPLIER_COLLECTS` Ã© o cliente pagando o fornecedor diretamente.
  Sem essa escolha nÃ£o se calcula fluxo de caixa.

### 6. Cancelamento, estorno e vÃ­nculo com quitaÃ§Ã£o

- Cancelar entrada em `FORECAST`, `ENTITLED` ou `AVAILABLE` â†’ `CANCELLED`, sem
  efeito financeiro.
- Cancelar venda com entrada `RECEIVED` â†’ **409** por padrÃ£o (Gadotti D6). NÃ£o hÃ¡
  clawback automÃ¡tico.
- Estorno de baixa do cliente que desfaz a quitaÃ§Ã£o, antes da liquidaÃ§Ã£o do
  fornecedor, devolve a entrada de `ENTITLED` para `FORECAST`.
- Entrada em lote fechado (`AVAILABLE` congelada) nÃ£o pode ser cancelada sem ajuste

+function fixture(restricted: boolean, restrictionError?: Error) {
+  const query = vi.fn(async (sql: string, _values?: readonly unknown[]) => {
+    if (sql.includes('FROM permission_restrictions')) {
+      if (restrictionError) throw restrictionError;
+      return { rows: restricted ? [{ id: 'restriction' }] : [], rowCount: restricted ? 1 : 0 };
+    }
+    if (sql.includes('FROM sales')) return { rows: [{}], rowCount: 1 };
+    if (sql.includes('FROM agency_revenue_entries')) {
+      return { rows: [{ id: 'revenue', status: 'RECEIVED', amount: 100, received_amount: 90 }], rowCount: 1 };
+    }
+    if (sql.includes('FROM commission_entries')) return { rows: [{ total: '25' }], rowCount: 1 };
+    throw new Error(`Unexpected query: ${sql}`);
+  });
+  const client = { query } as unknown as TenantTransactionClient;
+  const database: DatabaseRuntime = {
+    withTenantTransaction: (operation) => operation(client),
+    withPlatformTransaction: vi.fn(),
+  };
+  return { database, query };
+}

/** D6: cancelling a sale whose revenue the supplier already paid is blocked (409); the rest is cancelled. */
export async function cancelRevenueForSale(client: TenantTransactionClient, saleId: string): Promise<void> {
  if (!(await revenueSchemaPresent(client))) return;
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();
  await lockSale(client, saleId);
  const entries = await client.query<EntryRow>(
    `SELECT id, sale_item_id, status, entitlement_event, amount FROM agency_revenue_entries
      WHERE agency_id = $1 AND sale_id = $2 AND status <> 'CANCELLED' FOR UPDATE`,
    [agencyId, saleId],
  );
  if (entries.rows.some((entry) => entry.status === 'RECEIVED')) {
    throw new ConflictError('Cannot cancel a sale whose agency revenue was already received from the supplier');
  }
  for (const entry of entries.rows.filter((row) => row.status !== 'CANCELLED')) {
    await setStatus(client, entry.id, 'CANCELLED', AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_CANCELLED);
  }
}


async function lockEntrySale(client: TenantTransactionClient, agencyId: string, entryId: string): Promise<string> {
  const result = await client.query<{ sale_id: string }>(
    `SELECT sale_id FROM agency_revenue_entries WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2`, [agencyId, entryId]);
  const saleId = result.rows[0]?.sale_id;
  if (!saleId) throw new NotFoundError('Revenue entry not found');
  await lockSale(client, saleId);
  return saleId;
}

/** Item cancellation and the revenue transition belong to the caller's transaction. */
export async function cancelRevenueForItem(client: TenantTransactionClient, saleId: string, itemId: string): Promise<void> {
  if (!(await revenueSchemaPresent(client))) return;
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();
  await lockSale(client, saleId);
  const result = await client.query<EntryRow>(
    `SELECT id, sale_item_id, status, entitlement_event, amount FROM agency_revenue_entries
      WHERE agency_id = $1 AND sale_id = $2 AND sale_item_id = $3 AND status <> 'CANCELLED' FOR UPDATE`,
    [agencyId, saleId, itemId]);
  if (result.rows.some((entry) => entry.status === 'RECEIVED')) {
    throw new ConflictError('Cannot cancel an item whose agency revenue was already received');
  }
  for (const entry of result.rows) {
    await setStatus(client, entry.id, 'CANCELLED', AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_CANCELLED);
  }
}

export async function reactivateRevenueEntry(database: DatabaseRuntime, entryId: string, reason: string) {
  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();
  if (typeof reason !== 'string' || !reason.trim() || reason.trim().length > 128) throw new ValidationError('A reason of 1 to 128 characters is required');
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const saleId = await lockEntrySale(client, agencyId, entryId);
    const eligible = await client.query<EntryRow>(
      `SELECT e.id, e.sale_item_id, e.status, e.entitlement_event, e.amount FROM agency_revenue_entries e
       JOIN sale_items i ON i.agency_id=e.agency_id AND i.id=e.sale_item_id AND i.sale_id=e.sale_id
       JOIN sales s ON s.agency_id=e.agency_id AND s.id=e.sale_id
       WHERE e.agency_id=$1 AND e.id=$2 AND i.status='ACTIVE' AND s.status <> 'CANCELLED' FOR UPDATE OF e`,
      [agencyId, entryId]);
    const row = eligible.rows[0];
    if (!row || row.status !== 'CANCELLED') throw new ConflictError('Only cancelled revenue of an active item and sale can be reactivated');
    const live = await client.query(`SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries
      WHERE agency_id=$1 AND sale_item_id=$2 AND status <> 'CANCELLED'`, [agencyId, row.sale_item_id]);
    if (live.rowCount) throw new ConflictError('The item already has live revenue');
    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries SET
      status=CASE WHEN rule_version_id IS NULL OR memory ? 'reason' THEN 'PENDING_RULE' ELSE 'FORECAST' END,
      cancelled_at=NULL, entitled_at=NULL, updated_at=now() WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=$2`, [agencyId, entryId]);
    await recordAuditEvent(client, {
      eventType: AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_REACTIVATED, entityType: 'agency_revenue_entry', entityId: entryId,
      required: true,
      metadata: { reasonCode: reason.trim() },
    });
    await syncRevenueEntitlement(client, saleId);
    return { id: entryId };
  });
}

  database: DatabaseRuntime,
  entryId: string,
  input: { amount: number; reference: string | null },

const tenantStorage = new AsyncLocalStorage<TenantContext>();

export function runWithTenantContext<T>(
  context: TenantContext,
  callback: () => T,
): T {
  assertValidTenantContext(context);
  return tenantStorage.run(context, callback);
}

export function getOptionalTenantContext(): TenantContext | undefined {
  return tenantStorage.getStore();
}

+-- Grants de runtime ausentes nas migrations 103, 104, 108 e 109.
+-- Apenas os verbos usados pela API; tabelas append-only permanecem sem UPDATE/DELETE.
+-- GRANT pode ser repetido. As politicas RLS e os triggers permanecem inalterados.
+DO $$
+DECLARE role_name TEXT;
+BEGIN
+  FOREACH role_name IN ARRAY ARRAY['travel_app_runtime', 'travel_app_runtime_local'] LOOP
+    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = role_name) THEN
+      EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT, INSERT, DELETE ON user_permission_overrides TO %I', role_name);
+      EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON customer_field_settings, payment_method_receipt_rules, dashboard_layouts TO %I', role_name);
+      EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON commission_financial_profiles, commission_release_events TO %I', role_name);
+    END IF;
+  END LOOP;
+END $$;

+import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { runWithTenantContext } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
+import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
+import { getSaleRevenue } from '../src/agency-revenue';
+import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from '../src/database';
+
+function fixture(restricted: boolean, restrictionError?: Error) {
+  const query = vi.fn(async (sql: string, _values?: readonly unknown[]) => {
+    if (sql.includes('FROM permission_restrictions')) {
+      if (restrictionError) throw restrictionError;
+      return { rows: restricted ? [{ id: 'restriction' }] : [], rowCount: restricted ? 1 : 0 };
+    }
+    if (sql.includes('FROM sales')) return { rows: [{}], rowCount: 1 };
+    if (sql.includes('FROM agency_revenue_entries')) {
+      return { rows: [{ id: 'revenue', status: 'RECEIVED', amount: 100, received_amount: 90 }], rowCount: 1 };
+    }
+    if (sql.includes('FROM commission_entries')) return { rows: [{ total: '25' }], rowCount: 1 };
+    throw new Error(`Unexpected query: ${sql}`);
+  });
+  const client = { query } as unknown as TenantTransactionClient;
+  const database: DatabaseRuntime = {
+    withTenantTransaction: (operation) => operation(client),
+    withPlatformTransaction: vi.fn(),
+  };
+  return { database, query };
+}
+
+function readRevenue(database: DatabaseRuntime, role = UserRole.MANAGER) {
+  return runWithTenantContext(
+    { agencyId: 'agency', userId: 'user', userRole: role, email: 'user@example.test' },
+    () => getSaleRevenue(database, 'sale'),
+  );
+}
+
+describe('getSaleRevenue: employee commission restriction', () => {
+  it('omits commission and its derived difference for a restricted manager without querying commissions', async () => {
+    const { database, query } = fixture(true);
+    const result = await readRevenue(database);
+
+    expect(result.analysis).toEqual({ agencyRevenue: 100 });
+    expect(result.totals).toMatchObject({ expected: 100, received: 90, difference: 10 });
+    expect(result.entries).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(query.mock.calls.some(([sql]) => sql.includes('FROM commission_entries'))).toBe(false);
+    expect(query).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.stringContaining('FROM permission_restrictions'), [
+      'agency', UserRole.MANAGER, 'employees', 'view',
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it.each([UserRole.MANAGER, UserRole.ADMIN, UserRole.OWNER])('keeps the comparison for an authorized %s', async (role) => {
+    const { database, query } = fixture(role !== UserRole.MANAGER);
+    const result = await readRevenue(database, role);
+
+    expect(result.analysis).toEqual({ agencyRevenue: 100, employeeCommission: 25, difference: 75 });
+    expect(query).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.stringContaining('FROM commission_entries'), ['agency', 'sale']);
+  });
+
+  it('propagates restriction lookup errors instead of exposing commissions', async () => {
+    const error = new Error('Restriction lookup failed');
+    const { database, query } = fixture(false, error);
+
+    await expect(readRevenue(database)).rejects.toBe(error);
+    expect(query.mock.calls.some(([sql]) => sql.includes('FROM commission_entries'))).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it.each([UserRole.AGENT, UserRole.VIEWER])('keeps the MANAGER role floor for %s', async (role) => {
+    const { database, query } = fixture(false);
+
+    await expect(readRevenue(database, role)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'FORBIDDEN' });
+    expect(query).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+});

describe('Agency revenue (ADR-007) over HTTP with a real database', () => {
  let db: DisposableDb;
  let admin: Pool;
  const suppliers: Record<string, string> = {};

  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await startDisposableDb(MIGRATIONS.filter((name) => Number(name.slice(0, 3)) < 106));
    // Migration-owned grants: no bootstrap after creating the revenue tables.
    for (const name of MIGRATIONS.filter((file) => Number(file.slice(0, 3)) >= 106)) {
      await db.adminPool.query(readFileSync(resolve(import.meta.dirname, '../../../infrastructure/migrations', name), 'utf8').replace(/^\uFEFF/, ''));
    }
    admin = db.adminPool;

      `INSERT INTO agencies (id, name, slug, email, plan, status) VALUES
        ($1, 'Agency A', 'agency-a-rev', 'a@example.test', 'FREE', 'ACTIVE'),

      [agencyA, agencyB],
    );
    for (const [key, id] of Object.entries(ids)) {
      const [agencyId, role] = roleOf[key as keyof typeof ids];
      await admin.query(
        `INSERT INTO users (id, agency_id, email, name, role, password_hash, status)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, 'hash-test-only', 'ACTIVE')`,
        [id, agencyId, `${key}@example.test`, key, role],
      );
    }
    for (const name of ['A', 'B', 'C']) {
      suppliers[name] = (
        await admin.query<{ id: string }>(
          `INSERT INTO suppliers (agency_id, name) VALUES ($1, $2) RETURNING id`,
          [agencyA, `Fornecedor ${name}`],
        )
      ).rows[0]!.id;
    }
    customerA = (
      await admin.query<{ id: string }>(
        `INSERT INTO customers (agency_id, name, status) VALUES ($1, 'Cliente', 'ACTIVE') RETURNING id`,
        [agencyA],
      )
    ).rows[0]!.id;
  }, 300_000);

  afterAll(async () => {
    await db?.stop();
  });

  function app() {
    return buildApp({
    it('R1: 7% of 1,000.00 = 70.00 and stays FORECAST until the customer settles', async () => {
      await reset();
      await rule(ids.adminA, { supplierId: suppliers.A, mode: 'PERCENTAGE', baseSelector: 'LINE_TOTAL', value: 7 });
      const sale = await makeSale([{ supplier: 'A', unitPrice: 1000 }]);

      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      expect(res.body.entries).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(res.body.entries[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'FORECAST', amount: 70, base_amount: 1000 });
      expect(res.body.entries[0].memory).toMatchObject({ rate: 7, baseSelector: 'LINE_TOTAL', calcVersion: 1 });
      const condition = await call(ids.adminA, 'POST', '/payment-conditions', {
        name: 'R1', scheduleKind: 'EQUAL_INSTALLMENTS', maxInstallments: 1, minInstallmentAmount: null,
        interestFree: true, downPaymentMode: 'NONE', downPaymentValue: 0, downPaymentRequired: false,
        balanceDeadlineAnchor: 'SALE_DATE', balanceDeadlineOffsetDays: 0, allowsPartialPayments: true,
        paymentMethods: ['PIX'], categoryIds: [], productIds: [],
      });
      expect(condition.status).toBe(201);
      expect((await call(ids.adminA, 'POST', `/sales/${sale}/payment-schedule`, {
        conditionId: condition.body.condition.id, installments: 1, paymentMethod: 'PIX',
      })).status).toBe(201);
      expect((await call(ids.adminA, 'POST', `/sales/${sale}/payments`, {
        amount: 1000, idempotency_key: `r1-${sale}`, method: 'PIX', paid_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      })).status).toBe(201);
      const again = await call(ids.managerA, 'GET', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue`);
      expect(again.body.entries[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'ENTITLED', amount: 70 });
      expect(again.body.entries).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(today).toMatch(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/);
  });

  it('concurrent calculations and confirmation create exactly one entitlement', async () => {
    await reset();
    await rule(ids.adminA, { supplierId: suppliers.A, mode: 'PERCENTAGE', baseSelector: 'LINE_TOTAL', value: 10, entitlementEvent: 'ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION' });
    const sale = await makeSale([{ supplier: 'A', unitPrice: 1000 }], { status: 'PENDING' });
    const results = await Promise.all([
      call(ids.managerA, 'POST', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue/calculate`),
      call(ids.managerA, 'POST', `/sales/${sale}/confirm`),
      call(ids.managerA, 'POST', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue/calculate`),
    ]);
    expect(results.map((r) => r.status)).toEqual([200, 200, 200]);
    const read = await call(ids.managerA, 'GET', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue`);
    expect(read.body.entries).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(read.body.entries[0].status).toBe('ENTITLED');
    const audits = await admin.query<{ count: string }>(
      `SELECT count(*) FROM audit_logs WHERE entity_id=$1 AND event_type='AGENCY_REVENUE_ENTITLED'`, [read.body.entries[0].id]);
    expect(Number(audits.rows[0]?.count)).toBe(1);
  });

  it('audit failure rolls confirmation and entitlement back together', async () => {
    await reset();
    await rule(ids.adminA, { supplierId: suppliers.A, mode: 'PERCENTAGE', baseSelector: 'LINE_TOTAL', value: 10, entitlementEvent: 'ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION' });
    const sale = await makeSale([{ supplier: 'A', unitPrice: 1000 }], { status: 'PENDING' });
    await call(ids.managerA, 'POST', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue/calculate`);
    await admin.query(`CREATE FUNCTION reject_revenue_audit() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
      BEGIN IF NEW.event_type='AGENCY_REVENUE_ENTITLED' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'audit unavailable'; END IF; RETURN NEW; END $$;
      CREATE TRIGGER reject_revenue_audit BEFORE INSERT ON audit_logs FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION reject_revenue_audit()`);
    try {
      expect((await call(ids.managerA, 'POST', `/sales/${sale}/confirm`)).status).toBe(500);
      expect((await admin.query<{ status: string }>('SELECT status FROM sales WHERE id=$1', [sale])).rows[0]?.status).toBe('PENDING');
      expect((await call(ids.managerA, 'GET', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue`)).body.entries[0].status).toBe('FORECAST');
    } finally {
      await admin.query('DROP TRIGGER reject_revenue_audit ON audit_logs; DROP FUNCTION reject_revenue_audit()');
    }
  });

  it('item cancellation cancels its forecast and confirmation cannot entitle it', async () => {
    await reset();

    expect((await call(ids.managerA, 'GET', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue`)).body.entries[0].status).toBe('CANCELLED');
  });

  it('D6: received supplier revenue blocks item cancellation; totals use actual receipt', async () => {
    await reset();

    expect((await call(ids.adminA, 'POST', `/agency-revenue/entries/${entry.id}/receive`, { amount: 90 })).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await call(ids.adminA, 'POST', `/sale-items/${entry.sale_item_id}/cancel`)).status).toBe(409);
    const read = await call(ids.managerA, 'GET', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue`);
    expect(read.body.totals).toMatchObject({ expected: 100, received: 90, difference: 10 });
    expect((await admin.query<{ status: string }>('SELECT status FROM sale_items WHERE id=$1', [entry.sale_item_id])).rows[0]?.status).toBe('ACTIVE');
  });

  it('cancelled revenue survives recalculation and only explicit admin reactivation restores it', async () => {
    await reset();
    await rule(ids.adminA, { supplierId: suppliers.A, mode: 'PERCENTAGE', baseSelector: 'LINE_TOTAL', value: 10 });
    const sale = await makeSale([{ supplier: 'A', unitPrice: 1000 }], { status: 'PENDING' });
    const entry = (await call(ids.managerA, 'POST', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue/calculate`)).body.entries[0];
    expect((await call(ids.adminA, 'POST', `/agency-revenue/entries/${entry.id}/cancel`)).status).toBe(200);
    const recalculated = await call(ids.managerA, 'POST', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue/calculate`);
    expect(recalculated.body.entries).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(recalculated.body.entries[0].status).toBe('CANCELLED');
    expect((await call(ids.managerA, 'POST', `/agency-revenue/entries/${entry.id}/reactivate`, { reason: 'Revisao' })).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await call(ids.adminA, 'POST', `/agency-revenue/entries/${entry.id}/reactivate`, { reason: ' ' })).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await call(ids.adminB, 'POST', `/agency-revenue/entries/${entry.id}/reactivate`, { reason: 'Revisao' })).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await call(ids.adminA, 'POST', `/agency-revenue/entries/${entry.id}/reactivate`, { reason: 'Revisao comercial' })).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await call(ids.managerA, 'GET', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue`)).body.entries[0].status).toBe('FORECAST');
    const audit = await admin.query<{ metadata: { reasonCode: string } }>(
      `SELECT metadata FROM audit_logs WHERE entity_id=$1 AND event_type='AGENCY_REVENUE_REACTIVATED'`, [entry.id]);
    expect(audit.rows[0]?.metadata.reasonCode).toBe('Revisao comercial');
    expect((await call(ids.adminA, 'POST', `/agency-revenue/entries/${entry.id}/reactivate`, { reason: 'Duplicado' })).status).toBe(409);
  });

  it('legacy settlement synchronizes revenue without requiring settled_at', async () => {
    await reset();

    expect(payment.status).toBe(201);

      allocations: [{ receivableId: receipt.body.receivable.id, amount: 1000 }],
    })).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await call(ids.managerA, 'POST', `/sales/${sale}/mark-paid`)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await call(ids.managerA, 'GET', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue`)).body.entries[0].status).toBe('ENTITLED');
  });



  it('audit failure rolls explicit receipt and reactivation back', async () => {
    await reset();
    await rule(ids.adminA, { supplierId: suppliers.A,mode: 'PERCENTAGE',baseSelector: 'LINE_TOTAL',
      value: 10,entitlementEvent: 'ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION' });
    const sale = await makeSale([{ supplier: 'A',unitPrice: 1000 }]);
    const entry = (await call(ids.managerA, 'POST', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue/calculate`)).body.entries[0];
    await admin.query(`CREATE FUNCTION reject_revenue_transition_audit() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
      BEGIN IF NEW.event_type IN ('AGENCY_REVENUE_RECEIVED','AGENCY_REVENUE_REACTIVATED')
      THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'audit unavailable'; END IF; RETURN NEW; END $$;
      CREATE TRIGGER reject_revenue_transition_audit BEFORE INSERT ON audit_logs FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION reject_revenue_transition_audit()`);
    try {
      expect((await call(ids.adminA, 'POST', `/agency-revenue/entries/${entry.id}/receive`, { amount: 90 })).status).toBe(500);
      expect((await call(ids.managerA, 'GET', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue`)).body.entries[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'ENTITLED',received_amount: null });
      expect((await call(ids.adminA, 'POST', `/agency-revenue/entries/${entry.id}/cancel`)).status).toBe(200);

      expect((await call(ids.managerA, 'GET', `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue`)).body.entries[0].status).toBe('CANCELLED');
    } finally {
      await admin.query('DROP TRIGGER reject_revenue_transition_audit ON audit_logs; DROP FUNCTION reject_revenue_transition_audit()');
    }
  });

  it('D3: pays the employee commission through the financial flow before supplier receipt', async () => {

      value: 10, entitlementEvent: 'ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION', collectionModel: 'SUPPLIER_COLLECTS' });

    expect(revenue.body.totals.received).toBe(0);

  id: string,
): Promise<Sale | null> {
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();

  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const current = await client.query<SaleRow>(
      `SELECT ${SALE_COLUMNS}
       FROM sales
       WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2
       FOR UPDATE`,
      [agencyId, id],
    );
    const currentRow = current.rows[0];
    if (!currentRow) return null;

    if (currentRow.status === SaleStatus.PAID) {
      await syncRevenueEntitlement(client, id);
      return toSale(currentRow);
    }

    if (currentRow.status !== SaleStatus.CONFIRMED) {
      throw new ConflictError(`Cannot transition Sale from ${currentRow.status} to PAID`);
    }

    const total = Number(currentRow.total);
    if (total > 0) {
      const receivable = await getReceivablePaymentSummary(client, agencyId, id);
      if (!receivable || receivable.paidAmount < receivable.amount) {
        throw new ConflictError('Sale can be marked PAID only after its Receivable is fully paid');
      }
    }

    const updated = await client.query<SaleRow>(
      `UPDATE sales
       SET status = 'PAID', paid_at = now(), updated_at = now()
       WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2
       RETURNING ${SALE_COLUMNS}`,
      [agencyId, id],
    );

    const row = updated.rows[0];
    if (!row) throw new Error('Sale payment transition did not return a row');
    await syncRevenueEntitlement(client, id);
    return toSale(row);
  });
}

async function transitionSale(
  database: DatabaseRuntime,
  id: string,
  targetStatus: SaleStatus,
  allowedFrom: SaleStatus[],
): Promise<Sale | null> {
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();

  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const current = await client.query<SaleRow>(
      `SELECT ${SALE_COLUMNS}
       FROM sales
       WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2
       FOR UPDATE`,
      [agencyId, id],
    );
    const currentRow = current.rows[0];
    if (!currentRow) return null;

    if (currentRow.status === targetStatus) {
      await syncRevenueEntitlement(client, id);
      return toSale(currentRow);
    }

    if (!allowedFrom.includes(currentRow.status)) {
      throw new ConflictError(`Cannot transition Sale from ${currentRow.status} to ${targetStatus}`);
    }

    const updated = await client.query<SaleRow>(
      `UPDATE sales
       SET status = $3, updated_at = now()
       WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2
       RETURNING ${SALE_COLUMNS}`,
      [agencyId, id, targetStatus],
    );

    const row = updated.rows[0];
    if (!row) throw new Error('Sale transition did not return a row');
    await syncRevenueEntitlement(client, id);
    return toSale(row);
  });
}

async function getAllocatedReceivableAmount(
  client: TenantTransactionClient,
/**
 * Cancels a SaleItem and reverses its contribution to the Sale's
 * authoritative total via the same existing updateSale() path used to
 * add it. The linked Payable (if any) is left as-is -- payables.ts/
 * financial.ts expose no cancel-payable transition today, so reversing
 * a supplier cost is out of scope here (documented P2, not a workaround
 * with a hand-rolled formula).
 */
export async function cancelSaleItem(database: DatabaseRuntime, id: string): Promise<SaleItem | null> {
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();

  const existing = await getSaleItemById(database, id);
  if (!existing) return null;
  if (existing.status === 'CANCELLED') return existing;

  const row = await database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const locked = await client.query<{ amount: string }>(
      `SELECT amount FROM sales WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2 FOR UPDATE`,
      [agencyId, existing.saleId],
    );
    if (!locked.rows[0]) throw new NotFoundError('Sale not found');
    const saleAmount = Number(locked.rows[0].amount);
    await cancelRevenueForItem(client, existing.saleId, id);
    const result = await client.query<SaleItemRow>(
      `UPDATE sale_items SET status = 'CANCELLED', updated_at = now()
       WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2 AND status = 'ACTIVE'
       RETURNING ${SALE_ITEM_COLUMNS}`,
      [agencyId, id],
    );
    const updated = result.rows[0];
    if (!updated) return null;

    await recordAuditEvent(client, {
      eventType: AuditEventType.SALE_ITEM_CANCELLED,
      entityType: 'sale_item',
      entityId: updated.id,
      metadata: { saleId: updated.sale_id },
    });
  amount: number;
  base: number;
  memory: Record<string, unknown>;
}

interface Decimal { numerator: bigint; denominator: bigint }

function decimal(value: number): Decimal {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) throw new RangeError('Invalid monetary input');
  const [mantissa = '0', exponent = '0'] = String(value).toLowerCase().split('e');
  const [whole = '0', fraction = ''] = mantissa.split('.');
  const scale = fraction.length - Number(exponent);
  const numerator = BigInt(whole + fraction);
  return scale >= 0
    ? { numerator, denominator: 10n ** BigInt(scale) }
    : { numerator: numerator * 10n ** BigInt(-scale), denominator: 1n };
}

function multiply(a: Decimal, b: Decimal): Decimal {
  return { numerator: a.numerator * b.numerator, denominator: a.denominator * b.denominator };
}

function add(a: Decimal, b: Decimal): Decimal {
  return { numerator: a.numerator * b.denominator + b.numerator * a.denominator, denominator: a.denominator * b.denominator };
}

function subtractFloorZero(a: Decimal, b: Decimal): Decimal {
  const numerator = a.numerator * b.denominator - b.numerator * a.denominator;
  return { numerator: numerator > 0n ? numerator : 0n, denominator: a.denominator * b.denominator };
}

/** Round only the final item amount, using exact decimal half-up. */
function roundedCents(value: Decimal): number {
  const cents = (value.numerator * 200n + value.denominator) / (2n * value.denominator);
  const result = Number(cents);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(result)) throw new RangeError('Monetary input exceeds safe range');
  return result;
}

const toCents = (value: number): number => roundedCents(decimal(value));
const fromCents = (cents: number): number => cents / 100;

function percentOf(base: Decimal, percent: number): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(percent) || percent < 0 || percent > 100) throw new RangeError('Invalid percentage');
  const result = multiply(base, decimal(percent));
  return roundedCents({ numerator: result.numerator, denominator: result.denominator * 100n });
}

function itemBase(item: RevenueItemInput, selector: BaseSelector): Decimal {
  const fare = subtractFloorZero(multiply(decimal(item.qty), decimal(item.unitPrice)), decimal(item.discount));
  const taxes = decimal(item.taxes);
  const fees = decimal(item.fees);
  switch (selector) {
    case 'NET_FARE': return fare;
    case 'TAXES': return taxes;
    case 'FEES': return fees;
    case 'TAXES_AND_FEES': return add(taxes, fees);
    case 'MARGIN': return decimal(item.lineMargin);
    case 'LINE_TOTAL': return add(add(fare, taxes), fees);
  }
}

export function calculateSaleRevenue(items: readonly RevenueItemInput[]): RevenueEntryDraft[] {
  const appliedPerSale = new Set<string>();
  return items.map((item) => {
    const rule = item.rule;
    const pending = (reason: string, ruleVersionId: string | null): RevenueEntryDraft => ({
      itemId: item.itemId,
      supplierId: item.supplierId,
      ruleVersionId,
      status: 'PENDING_RULE',
      amount: 0,
      base: 0,
      memory: { calcVersion: REVENUE_CALC_VERSION, reason },
    });
    if (item.supplierId === null) return pending('NO_SUPPLIER', null);
    if (!rule) return pending('NO_APPLICABLE_RULE', null);
    if (!Number.isFinite(rule.value) || rule.value < 0) throw new RangeError('Invalid rule value');

    const memory: Record<string, unknown> = {
      calcVersion: REVENUE_CALC_VERSION,
      ruleVersionId: rule.ruleVersionId,
      mode: rule.mode,
      baseSelector: rule.baseSelector,
      collectionModel: rule.collectionModel,
    };
    let base = 0;
    let cents = 0;
    switch (rule.mode) {
      case 'PERCENTAGE': {
        if (!rule.baseSelector) return pending('RULE_WITHOUT_BASE', rule.ruleVersionId);
        if (rule.baseSelector === 'MARGIN' && rule.collectionModel !== 'AGENCY_COLLECTS') {
          return pending('MARGIN_REQUIRES_AGENCY_COLLECTS', rule.ruleVersionId);
        }
        const selectedBase = itemBase(item, rule.baseSelector);
        cents = percentOf(selectedBase, rule.value);
        base = fromCents(roundedCents(selectedBase));
        memory.rate = rule.value;
        break;
      }
      case 'FIXED_PER_PASSENGER':
        if (!Number.isInteger(item.passengerCount) || item.passengerCount < 0) {
          throw new RangeError('Invalid passenger count');
        }
        cents = roundedCents(multiply(decimal(rule.value), decimal(item.passengerCount)));
        memory.passengerCount = item.passengerCount;
        memory.unitAmount = rule.value;
        break;
      case 'FIXED_PER_ITEM':
        cents = toCents(rule.value);
        break;
      case 'FIXED_PER_SALE':
        // Once per rule version across the sale: the first matching item carries it.
        if (!appliedPerSale.has(rule.ruleVersionId)) {
          appliedPerSale.add(rule.ruleVersionId);
          cents = toCents(rule.value);
        } else {
          memory.coveredByEarlierItem = true;
        }
        break;

+import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

+function fixture(restricted: boolean, restrictionError?: Error) {
+  const query = vi.fn((sql: string, _values?: readonly unknown[]) => {
+    if (sql.includes('FROM permission_restrictions')) {
+      if (restrictionError) return Promise.reject(restrictionError);
+      return Promise.resolve({ rows: restricted ? [{ id: 'restriction' }] : [], rowCount: restricted ? 1 : 0 });

+    if (sql.includes('FROM sales')) return Promise.resolve({ rows: [{}], rowCount: 1 });

+      return Promise.resolve({ rows: [{ id: 'revenue', status: 'RECEIVED', amount: 100, received_amount: 90 }], rowCount: 1 });
+    }
+    if (sql.includes('FROM commission_entries')) return Promise.resolve({ rows: [{ total: '25' }], rowCount: 1 });
+    throw new Error(`Unexpected query: ${sql}`);
+  });
+  const client = { query } as unknown as TenantTransactionClient;
+  const database: DatabaseRuntime = {
+    withTenantTransaction: (operation) => operation(client),
+    withPlatformTransaction: vi.fn(),
+  };
+  return { database, query };
+}
+
+function readRevenue(database: DatabaseRuntime, role = UserRole.MANAGER) {
+  return runWithTenantContext(
+    { agencyId: 'agency', userId: 'user', userRole: role, email: 'user@example.test' },
+    () => getSaleRevenue(database, 'sale'),
+  );
+}
+
+describe('getSaleRevenue: employee commission restriction', () => {
+  it('omits commission and its derived difference for a restricted manager without querying commissions', async () => {
+    const { database, query } = fixture(true);
+    const result = await readRevenue(database);
+
+    expect(result.analysis).toEqual({ agencyRevenue: 100 });
+    expect(result.totals).toMatchObject({ expected: 100, received: 90, difference: 10 });
+    expect(result.entries).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(query.mock.calls.some(([sql]) => sql.includes('FROM commission_entries'))).toBe(false);
+    expect(query).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.stringContaining('FROM permission_restrictions'), [
+      'agency', UserRole.MANAGER, 'employees', 'view',
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it.each([UserRole.MANAGER, UserRole.ADMIN, UserRole.OWNER])('keeps the comparison for an authorized %s', async (role) => {
+    const { database, query } = fixture(role !== UserRole.MANAGER);
+    const result = await readRevenue(database, role);
+
+    expect(result.analysis).toEqual({ agencyRevenue: 100, employeeCommission: 25, difference: 75 });
+    expect(query).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.stringContaining('FROM commission_entries'), ['agency', 'sale']);
+  });
+
+  it('propagates restriction lookup errors instead of exposing commissions', async () => {
+    const error = new Error('Restriction lookup failed');
+    const { database, query } = fixture(false, error);
+
+    await expect(readRevenue(database)).rejects.toBe(error);
+    expect(query.mock.calls.some(([sql]) => sql.includes('FROM commission_entries'))).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it.each([UserRole.AGENT, UserRole.VIEWER])('keeps the MANAGER role floor for %s', async (role) => {
+    const { database, query } = fixture(false);
+
+    await expect(readRevenue(database, role)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'FORBIDDEN' });
+    expect(query).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+});

function sanitizeMetadataString(value: string): string | undefined {
  const normalized = value.trim();
  return normalized.length > 0 && normalized.length <= maxMetadataStringLength
    ? normalized
    : undefined;
}

function normalizeLimit(limit: number | undefined): number {
  if (!Number.isInteger(limit) || limit === undefined) {
    return 100;
  }

  return Math.min(Math.max(limit, 1), 100);
}

function toAuditLogEntry(row: AuditLogRow): AuditLogEntry {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    occurredAt: new Date(row.occurred_at),
    agencyId: row.agency_id,
    actorType: row.actor_type,
    eventType: row.event_type,
    entityType: row.entity_type,
    outcome: row.outcome,
    metadata: row.metadata,
    ...(row.actor_id ? { actorId: row.actor_id } : {}),
    ...(row.entity_id ? { entityId: row.entity_id } : {}),
  };
}
