-- Entries move through states: UPDATE only there. Scopes and rule versions are append-only.
CREATE POLICY agency_revenue_entries_update_tenant ON agency_revenue_entries
  FOR UPDATE USING (agency_id = current_agency_id()) WITH CHECK (agency_id = current_agency_id());

DO $$
DECLARE role_name TEXT;
BEGIN
  FOREACH role_name IN ARRAY ARRAY['travel_app_runtime', 'travel_app_runtime_local'] LOOP
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = role_name) THEN
      EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON agency_revenue_scopes, agency_revenue_rule_versions TO %I', role_name);
      EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON agency_revenue_entries TO %I', role_name);
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
END $$;
/** Entries cannot exist before migration 106, so the sale hooks are a no-op on a schema without it. */
async function revenueSchemaPresent(client: TenantTransactionClient): Promise<boolean> {
  const result = await client.query<{ present: boolean }>(`SELECT to_regclass('public.agency_revenue_entries') IS NOT NULL AS present`);
  return result.rows[0]?.present === true;
}

export async function syncRevenueEntitlement(client: TenantTransactionClient, saleId: string): Promise<void> {
  if (!(await revenueSchemaPresent(client))) return;
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();
  const sale = await client.query<{ status: string; settled_at: Date | null }>(
    `SELECT status::text AS status, settled_at FROM sales WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2 FOR UPDATE`,
    [agencyId, saleId],
  );
  const row = sale.rows[0];
  if (!row || row.status === 'CANCELLED') return;
  const confirmed = row.status === 'CONFIRMED' || row.status === 'PAID';
  const settled = row.settled_at !== null || row.status === 'PAID';
  const entries = await client.query<EntryRow>(
    `SELECT id, sale_item_id, status, entitlement_event, amount FROM agency_revenue_entries
      WHERE agency_id = $1 AND sale_id = $2 AND status IN ('FORECAST', 'ENTITLED')
        AND sale_item_id IN (SELECT id FROM sale_items WHERE agency_id = $1 AND sale_id = $2 AND status = 'ACTIVE') FOR UPDATE`,
    [agencyId, saleId],
  );
  for (const entry of entries.rows) {
    const due = entry.entitlement_event === 'ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION' ? confirmed : confirmed && settled;
    if (due && entry.status === 'FORECAST') {
      await setStatus(client, entry.id, 'ENTITLED', AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_ENTITLED);
    } else if (!due && entry.status === 'ENTITLED') {
      await setStatus(client, entry.id, 'FORECAST', AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_ENTITLED);
    }

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { runWithTenantContext } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
import { getSaleRevenue } from '../src/agency-revenue';
import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from '../src/database';

function fixture(restricted: boolean, restrictionError?: Error) {
  const query = vi.fn((sql: string, _values?: readonly unknown[]) => {
    if (sql.includes('FROM permission_restrictions')) {
      if (restrictionError) return Promise.reject(restrictionError);
      return Promise.resolve({ rows: restricted ? [{ id: 'restriction' }] : [], rowCount: restricted ? 1 : 0 });
    }
    if (sql.includes('FROM sales')) return Promise.resolve({ rows: [{}], rowCount: 1 });
    if (sql.includes('FROM agency_revenue_entries')) {
      return Promise.resolve({ rows: [{ id: 'revenue', status: 'RECEIVED', amount: 100, received_amount: 90 }], rowCount: 1 });
    }
    if (sql.includes('FROM commission_entries')) return Promise.resolve({ rows: [{ total: '25' }], rowCount: 1 });
    throw new Error(`Unexpected query: ${sql}`);
  });
  const client = { query } as unknown as TenantTransactionClient;
  const database: DatabaseRuntime = {
    withTenantTransaction: (operation) => operation(client),
    withPlatformTransaction: vi.fn(),
  };
  return { database, query };
}

function readRevenue(database: DatabaseRuntime, role = UserRole.MANAGER) {
  return runWithTenantContext(
    { agencyId: 'agency', userId: 'user', userRole: role, email: 'user@example.test' },
    () => getSaleRevenue(database, 'sale'),
  );
}

describe('getSaleRevenue: employee commission restriction', () => {
  it('omits commission and its derived difference for a restricted manager without querying commissions', async () => {
    const { database, query } = fixture(true);
    const result = await readRevenue(database);

    expect(result.analysis).toEqual({ agencyRevenue: 100 });
    expect(result.totals).toMatchObject({ expected: 100, received: 90, difference: 10 });
    expect(result.entries).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(query.mock.calls.some(([sql]) => sql.includes('FROM commission_entries'))).toBe(false);
    expect(query).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.stringContaining('FROM permission_restrictions'), [
      'agency', UserRole.MANAGER, 'employees', 'view',
    ]);
  });

  it.each([UserRole.MANAGER, UserRole.ADMIN, UserRole.OWNER])('keeps the comparison for an authorized %s', async (role) => {
    const { database, query } = fixture(role !== UserRole.MANAGER);
    const result = await readRevenue(database, role);

    expect(result.analysis).toEqual({ agencyRevenue: 100, employeeCommission: 25, difference: 75 });
    expect(query).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.stringContaining('FROM commission_entries'), ['agency', 'sale']);
  });

  it('propagates restriction lookup errors instead of exposing commissions', async () => {
    const error = new Error('Restriction lookup failed');
    const { database, query } = fixture(false, error);

    await expect(readRevenue(database)).rejects.toBe(error);
    expect(query.mock.calls.some(([sql]) => sql.includes('FROM commission_entries'))).toBe(false);
  });

  it.each([UserRole.AGENT, UserRole.VIEWER])('keeps the MANAGER role floor for %s', async (role) => {
    const { database, query } = fixture(false);

    await expect(readRevenue(database, role)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'FORBIDDEN' });
    expect(query).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
  return result.rows;
}

/** Read model: revenue entries plus an analysis-only comparison with the employee commission (no netting). */
export async function getSaleRevenue(database: DatabaseRuntime, saleId: string) {
  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const sale = await client.query(`SELECT 1 FROM sales WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2`, [getAgencyId(), saleId]);
    if (sale.rowCount === 0) throw new NotFoundError('Sale not found');
    const entries = await loadEntries(client, saleId);
    let canViewCommission = true;
    try {
      await assertNotRestricted(client, getTenantContext().userRole, 'employees', 'view');
    } catch (error) {
      if (!(error instanceof ForbiddenError)) throw error;
      canViewCommission = false;
    }
    let employeeCommission: number | undefined;
    if (canViewCommission) {
      const commission = await client.query<{ total: string }>(
        `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS total FROM commission_entries
          WHERE agency_id = $1 AND sale_id = $2 AND status <> 'CANCELLED'`,
        [getAgencyId(), saleId],
      );
      employeeCommission = Math.round(Number(commission.rows[0]?.total ?? 0) * 100) / 100;
    }
    const sum = (statuses: string[], field: 'amount' | 'received_amount' = 'amount') =>
      Math.round(entries.filter((entry) => statuses.includes(entry.status as string)).reduce((total, entry) => total + Math.round(Number(entry[field] ?? 0) * 100), 0)) / 100;
    const projected = sum(['FORECAST', 'ENTITLED', 'AVAILABLE', 'RECEIVED']);
    const expectedReceived = sum(['RECEIVED']);
    const actualReceived = sum(['RECEIVED'], 'received_amount');
    return {
      entries,
      totals: {
        forecast: sum(['FORECAST']),
        entitled: sum(['ENTITLED', 'AVAILABLE']),
        expected: expectedReceived,
        received: actualReceived,
        difference: Math.round((expectedReceived - actualReceived) * 100) / 100,
        pendingRule: entries.filter((entry) => entry.status === 'PENDING_RULE').length,
      },
      // Analysis only (D3): two independent obligations, never netted or linked by payment.
      analysis: {
        agencyRevenue: projected,
        // Omitir tambem a diferenca: ela permitiria deduzir a comissao restrita.
        ...(employeeCommission === undefined ? {} : {
          employeeCommission,
          difference: Math.round((projected - employeeCommission) * 100) / 100,
        }),
      },
    };
  });
}
